## Missing/unresolved references, recursion, and compatibility

* Every edge above resolves within its own population. Calls not resolving as project functions were screened as platform/built-in calls; ambiguous lowercase unresolved names require runtime coverage and are not certified as missing solely by regex.
* Direct self-recursion: none detected.
* Argument compatibility was reviewed statically by declaration arity. Apps Script/JavaScript permits omitted/extra arguments; semantic compatibility remains **NOT VERIFIED** without execution.
* Return-value and error propagation are recorded in source bodies but cannot be conclusively certified across every dynamic branch without runtime tests.
